## Summary

OpenAI is the San Francisco AI lab behind ChatGPT, the GPT model series, and a fast-expanding product surface (Sora for video, [[agentkit]] for agents, [[openai-codex]] now reframed as a ChatGPT+Atlas+Codex superapp). Through October 2025 it became the most valuable private company at a reported $500B valuation, hosted DevDay, and locked in multi-silicon compute via [[amd]] and [[broadcom]]. The late-October to early-November 2025 window saw a step-change: it shipped the [[chatgpt-atlas]] browser, [[chatgpt-company-knowledge]] for workplace RAG, the [[aardvark]] GPT-5 security agent, and Sora 2 cameos / monetization; closed a $22.5B SoftBank tranche, completed its for-profit (PBC) recapitalization with renewed Microsoft terms (~27% stake), and reportedly began exploring a $1T IPO; signed a $38B multi-year compute deal with [[amazon]] AWS; and laid out an explicit AGI roadmap. By April 2026 the Codex surface had absorbed background computer use, parallel agents, an in-app browser, and image generation, and OpenAI shipped a biology-tuned specialty model, GPT-Rosalind, aimed at drug discovery. Its Trusted Access program continued to expand, and the Agents SDK picked up a native harness and sandboxed execution.

First publicly framed *Anthropic + OpenAI Foundation co-anchored health-philanthropy fund* in this wiki — structurally significant: the two leading frontier labs are co-investing in a public-health initiative outside their core AI commercialization arcs, framing the AI industry's institutional posture as broader than per-lab product cadence. (3) **[[openai-codex]] CLI logging bug silently writing ~37TB / 21 days to some users' SSDs**: Mindstream "Codex is silently killing your SSD" lands ***"A logging bug in OpenAI's Codex CLI may be causing significant strain on some users' SSDs, with one user recording around 37TB of writes in 21 days, potentially reaching 640TB in a year. The issue is still open on GitHub"***. First publicly framed *frontier-coding-agent disk-write pathology* in this wiki — productizes the broader *agent-as-resource-consumer* concern: heavy logging in agent loops can shred consumer SSDs at rates that exceed normal write-endurance specs (3D NAND consumer SSDs typically rated 150–600 TBW; 640 TB/year would burn through a mid-tier consumer SSD's TBW budget in roughly 1–4 years). Structurally significant for developer-tooling reliability — agents that loop over tool-calls + thinking-traces need to log responsibly. The four components are structurally distinct surfaces: (a) **Codex Security plugin** — productizes [[codex-security]] as a *plugin* within the broader Codex ecosystem (composable inside ChatGPT / Codex App / API), (b) **full GPT-5.5-Cyber model for trusted defenders** — see [[gpt-5-5-cyber]] (NEW page), record 85.6% on CyberGym test, generally available to verified defenders who can run it inside their own products, (c) **Cyber Partner Program** — partner-program commercial structure for OpenAI's cybersec surface (first such in this wiki), structurally analog to Anthropic's [[project-glasswing]] but with explicit *partner-program* commercial wrapper, (d) **Patch the Planet** — named open-source-flaw-fixing initiative (first such in this wiki for OpenAI), explicitly targets critical OSS using AI for vulnerability discovery + remediation. Evolving AI Insights frames the expansion as the cycle-competitive response to [[anthropic]]: ***"This move is part of the race between OpenAI and Anthropic to secure the world's software, with Anthropic having made a similar move first with its restricted Mythos model in April"*** — first publicly framed *direct OpenAI-vs-Anthropic competitive narrative* on the cybersec-AI axis in this wiki. The verified-defender embedded-deployment model graduates OpenAI's cybersec-AI distribution into an *enterprise-OEM* surface: vendors integrate GPT-5.5-Cyber as the underlying capability driver inside their own proprietary cybersec offerings. This initiative aims to give the public a stake in the companies and a share of their profits, as these companies have been built using public data and labor"***. - **2026-06-19-morning**: **LifeSciBench launch + ChatGPT market-share-dips-below-50% Sensor Tower number (46.4%) + ChatGPT task-scheduling polish + Pulse retired confirmation + second-cohort Noam Shazeer hire framing**. (1) **TLDR AI "ChatGPT marketshare drops 📉, Vercel eve 🤖, Replit links Claude 🔌"** carries the cycle's load-bearing OpenAI signals: (a) **OpenAI introduces LifeSciBench, an expert-judged benchmark that evaluates AI systems on end-to-end life sciences workflows** — first publicly framed LifeSciBench surface in this wiki; positions OpenAI as the *evaluator-of-record* for life-sciences workflows (extends [[gpt-rosalind]]'s biology-tuned-model surface from a *capability* into a *benchmark suite*). The "expert-judged" + "end-to-end workflows" framing is structurally distinct from synthetic-benchmark + single-task benchmarks (e.g. MMLU-Bio, ChemBench) — life-sciences workflows by definition involve multi-step planning, wet-lab integration, regulatory considerations, expert review of outputs that won't be easily covered by automated evaluations. LifeSciBench reads as OpenAI staking out the *benchmark-as-moat* position in the same way it did with HumanEval (coding) and MMLU (knowledge) — by *publishing* the benchmark, OpenAI shapes the evaluation criteria the rest of the field optimizes against. (b) **ChatGPT has improved its task scheduling feature, enhancing speed and reliability, and has retired Pulse** — confirms the 2026-06-18-evening "Pulse sunset within 14 days" framing has now resolved into a *completed retirement* (Pulse is gone, the scheduled-tasks hub fills the slot) and adds a *quality-improvement* signal on the new tasks surface (speed/reliability gains within hours of launch). (c) **ChatGPT market share dips below 50% for the first time** at ~**46.4%** by May 2026 per Sensor Tower's State of AI Report, vs **Gemini 27.7% (662M MAUs)** and **Claude 10.3% (245M MAUs)**. While ChatGPT's absolute user base is still growing, the share-decline narrative pressures OpenAI's pricing, product roadmaps, and potential IPO plans — first publicly framed concrete cross-product market-share number for ChatGPT in this wiki since the 2026-06-05 "1B MAU" framing (the 46.4% denominator implies a ~2.4B-user AI-assistant TAM as the global landscape). Analysts link this shift to intensified competition + subscription-conversion differences + user reactions to OpenAI's monetization moves (ads) + multi-assistant experimentation. - **2026-06-18-evening**: **ChatGPT Scheduled Tasks launch (replaces Pulse) + $150M Partner Network + Noam Shazeer hire from Google + Q1 2026 cash burn data + G7 attendance**. The cycle's load-bearing OpenAI signals: (1) **World of AI "Snap's $2,195 AI glasses 👓, ChatGPT schedules your life ⏰, Seedance 2.5 video is coming 🎬"** lands the **ChatGPT Scheduled Tasks hub** launch — see [[chatgpt-tasks]]. Users can set **one-off or recurring tasks** at a specific time or across a window like *morning* or *evening*; ChatGPT can now **proactively check the web or connected apps and ping the user**. The launch **replaces Pulse** (the daily-summary feature), which is **sunset within 14 days**. World of AI explicitly frames this as a paradigm shift from *assistant-on-demand* to *agent-on-a-schedule* — turning ChatGPT into a background worker. Joins [[anthropic]]'s scheduled Claude agents (2026-06-11-morning), Grok automations (2026-06-16-morning), and Perplexity emails-you-research (2026-06-16-morning) into a now-four-lab cross-cycle convergence on always-on scheduled agents (see [[agent-frameworks]]) — Q2-2026 is the cross-lab pivot quarter from session-based pull-AI to scheduled/triggered push-AI. (2) **OpenAI launches its $150M Partner Network to scale enterprise distribution** (per researchFindings.missingStories + Evolving AI Insights coverage) — the company's **first formal channel program**, backed by a $150M investment supporting solution providers and systems integrators to build, market, and deploy applications on top of OpenAI's models for enterprise customers. Marks a shift toward a more mature enterprise GTM model (analogous to traditional SaaS ecosystems) — for AI practitioners, means more standardized reference architectures + partner-built vertical solutions + clearer pathways to bring custom AI applications into large organizations via certified intermediaries. (3) **Evolving AI Insights "🩻 Midjourney Bets Its Scanner Can PREVENT 30% of Deaths"** carries the **Noam Shazeer hire from Google to OpenAI** — Google has *lost its $2.7B Transformer genius*; Shazeer co-wrote the original 2017 Transformer paper and had been working on Google's Gemini project. His departure is framed as a significant loss for Google + key talent gain for OpenAI as it prepares for IPO. **World of AI** adjacent line: "departure of Noam Shazeer from Google to OpenAI, indicating industry churn and competition for top AI talent". First publicly framed Shazeer departure in this wiki — see [[google]] for the Google-side framing. (4) **The Rundown AI "🔒 Inside the deadlock keeping Mythos offline"** carries OpenAI's Q1 2026 financial picture: **$3.7B in Q1 cash burn against $5.7B in revenue in 2026, with both *tripling* from a year earlier** — first publicly framed concrete OpenAI quarterly burn-vs-revenue numbers in this wiki (sharpens the 2026-06-09-evening "~40%→50% business revenue by EOY" trajectory with a hard burn-rate denominator). Cash burn keeping pace with revenue growth is the cleanest cross-cycle signal that OpenAI's IPO pricing depends on the revenue-trajectory holding through 2027 — not on near-term cash flow. (5) **Uncovering AI "🌍 The G7 just drew battle lines for AI control"** + **The Rundown AI** confirm **Sam Altman attended the G7 summit at Evian alongside Dario Amodei + Demis Hassabis** to discuss AI control and regulation with world leaders, proposing a "U.S.-led AI coalition" to address model access, chip exports, and safety risks. ## Key Facts

- Flagship surfaces: ChatGPT/GPT family, [[sora-2]], [[agentkit]], [[openai-codex]] (Apr 2026 superapp), [[chatgpt-atlas]] browser, [[chatgpt-company-knowledge]], [[aardvark]] security agent
- Specialty model: GPT-Rosalind (biology / drug discovery, Apr 2026)
- Reported $500B valuation (Oct 2025); $1T IPO exploration (Oct 30 2025)
- Compute partners: [[amd]] (GPUs), [[broadcom]] (custom silicon), [[amazon]] AWS ($38B multi-year, Nov 2025)
- Major investors / partners: SoftBank ($22.5B Oct 27 2025); Microsoft (~27% renewed Oct 29 2025); PayPal (commerce)
- Alumni spinouts: [[thinking-machines]]
- External pressure: Japan (Sora IP), 18+ content policy change, Hollywood coverage, Ilya Sutskever deposition (Nov 2025)
